# Whole-vision accountability, October 7, 2026

Status: exhaustive entry consideration and derived traceability, not whole-product completion. The [coordination owner](COORDINATION.md#recurring-continuation-authorized-october-4) owns execution, ranking, scheduler and packet results. The [binding course contract](../../SOURCE-TO-COURSE.md), [original vision](../../USER-VISION.md) and [permanent idea ledger](../../IDEA-LEDGER.md) retain their different authorities. This report interprets existing records and does not accept a new format, feature, score, source grant or learner artifact.

## Denominator and actual reading

All **93 dated USER-VISION entries** are accounted for below. Three bounded readers read every raw body and applicable interpretation/update in disjoint groups of 31, comparing them with the pinned canonical bytes. The coordinator read every extracted original blockquote and the full source-to-course contract, integrated every entry's meaning, relationship, gap and next gate, and inspected focused implementation symbols for the selected packet. Large source modules were sampled by symbols, not audited whole. Older research was consulted at each cited scope; its entire archives and every donor implementation were not reread or retested.

These are entries, not 93 features or 93 literal quotations. V20 includes a boilerplate quote template inside a parser boundary, which is excluded as user intent. V31 records photograph-derived item shapes without a verbatim blockquote; it does not authorize reconstructing third-party questions. Seven old entries have interpretation pointers rather than full adjacent interpretations. Ten interpretations lack an explicit relationship field; derived relationships below do not rewrite the originals.

The initial structural audit found **93 vision entries, 97 inbox entries, 32 exact downstream links and 61 without exact links**, ten unresolved named-file occurrences and four ambiguous roots. A later independent capture added the [practice-efficiency report](../../PROBLEM-LEDGER.md#p-20261007-01-practice-feels-less-efficient-than-generating-problems-in-chat), bringing the inbox to **98**. Its exact observation was read and retained. That report is advice/exploration, with timing and cause unverified; it does not accept a chat-primary redesign. Canonical USER-VISION remained byte-identical to its starting SHA-256, `be6bdffe6167dfb7574a7d7715fbea8faf9abd68bd43fb5d1cf59669083820a0`.

Every exact vision link below accompanies an interpreted outcome, owner, scoped evidence, unresolved remainder and next gate. The resulting link count measures accountability, not delivery. [FEATURE-INVENTORY](../../FEATURE-INVENTORY.md) maps 604 numbered donor observations to 27 capability groups, not 604 missing features. Its older unnumbered research coverage remains unresolved. Requirement checkboxes, phase freezes and test totals do not establish current installed or human acceptance.

Local inputs, full lane rows, read ranges, pin/drift checks, journal and before-images are retained under ignored `.reasonix/product-gm-20261007/vision/`. Each row distinguishes current inspection from dated reported execution. No new external-source claims were researched in this mapping pass. A later experiment must refresh the specific relevant primary source instead of treating a dated comparison as current.

## Reconciliation that changes decisions

R1: The [August 27 percent ruling](../../USER-VISION.md#2026-08-27-percent-is-permitted-and-user-vision-outranks-a-contract-clause) and GRAPH-03 amendment supersede the earlier blanket ban on aggregates/percent. This permission is settled. Older no-aggregate wording is stale owner prose, not a renewed veto. Honest underlying dimensions, authority, scope and denominators remain required; a particular aggregation rule still needs its own support.

R2: OCR extraction does not fulfill the full paper-note enhancement job. The inspected flat-output adapter reports unknown line confidence and bounding boxes. Keep unknowns honest; do not invent measurements to satisfy the registered per-line confidence goal. Exact correction, duplicate-scan reconciliation, source-backed enhancement and understanding checks remain separate gates.

R3: Broad subscription-replacement ambition remains broader than UI similarity. NotebookLM's earlier research-only interpretation and later capability target must stay visible. General companion health/open-tab behavior does not prove course-specific notebook identity, subscription transport or reviewed transfer. The current mocked health repair supersedes the old health-response narrative only at that seam.

R4: Original force-highlighting consideration, never-scored selected-response commitments and optional elimination reasoning retain their exact dispositions. Optional note strategies, pending prose, own-picks feedback and scored staged MC do not silently satisfy those different jobs. Preserve their retained alternatives and semantic/denominator gates.

R5: The requested auditable project timeline remains distinct from the course-overview specimen at `prototypes/history-ui-20261003/`. Older unavailable Search/Notes/Agent statements are historical where later source work supplies those doors. Neither correction establishes full authoring, teaching or visual completion.

## Seven joined loops and five GM dimensions

| Loop | Scoped baseline reused | Remaining acceptance surface |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A discover, reconcile and bind | Approved-root inventory, source adapters and current source search/context owners; the [October 5 source-search continuation](../in-depth-improvement-2026-10-05.md) records exact Unicode return and rights refusal. | Normal new-course intake, adequate-artifact reuse and reviewed binding across synthetic roots, with unavailable/conflict states. |
| B design a course | P12 native structural proposal preserves IDs, prerequisites, bindings and history through review/restart/Undo. | Representative scope/demand fidelity and treatment quality, beyond one order edit. |
| C learn and construct notes | P1/P2 reader note and deep-passage recovery, P7 media reflow and the released UI/interaction reports. | Equivalent explanation depth across math, coding and language; useful static meaning and human accessibility/preference. |
| D practice and test | P9 pending-prose status, P11 historical review, existing runtime disclosure and the [coding-learning unit](../coding-learning-2026-10-01.md). | Efficient normal entry, targeted adaptation, faithful difficulty/format, misconception feedback and fresh transfer. |
| E evidence and remediation | Runtime-owned marks and withholding, explicit historical/pending/corrupt distinctions, exact review return. | A justified next activity with real denominators; no inference that widget use establishes learning or mastery. |
| F author, review and accept | P3 durable author controls, P10 stale-base refusals, P12 common outline decision gate. | P13 useful stale-review return, then source-to-accepted-artifact authoring quality and provider parity at their actual gates. |
| G maintain, recover and leave | P4/P5 original-work and restore return, P8 offline media transport and retained clean/union-copy restore reports. | Delivery candidate, cold installed use and complete offline restore/loss reporting; in-place generation coordination remains open. |

The first finite wave covers learning through W2/W3, experience through W1/W2/W4, functionality through W1/W3, reliability through W1/W5, and maintainability through W1/W5 and this derived map. The ranked wave and its observable acceptance gates live in COORDINATION, avoiding a second queue here.

## Quality gate and reviewable pilot

Use one joined fictional course and sample three cognitive demands: math explanation/changed-case reasoning, coding prediction/repair/transfer, and language meaning/use in a changed context. Reuse adequate existing fixtures, including the coding boundary unit; do not infer all three from a quiz or replace direct reading by default. This is a planned finite quality experiment, not a completed course or pilot.

| Quality dimension | Observable gate |
| --- | --- |
| Source fidelity and alignment | Every instructional claim identifies its source/locator or visible synthetic synthesis; the treatment and assessment match the reviewed objective and demand. |
| Explanation and prerequisites | A learner can explain why the rule works, identify its assumptions and apply the prerequisite in the next step; an attractive reveal is insufficient. |
| Misconception feedback | A deliberately wrong synthetic response receives only runtime-released feedback that distinguishes the misconception and provides a usable correction. Prose remains pending. |
| Transfer | A new situation changes relevant surface details or conditions; measure unaided explanation/application without copying a worked answer. |
| Next action and continuity | The next activity states its evidence, uncertainty and denominator, allows learner choice, and returns/restarts at the exact task without replay or invented mastery. |

Prepare a local review sheet recording task completion, time to first useful problem, setup actions, explanation/correction quality, unaided transfer and next-day return. Compare the same source/topic/demand/model for app versus chat only when those inputs are actually available; do not invent a baseline or use a paid live provider. A person supplies satisfaction/learning and representative keyboard, physical touch, screen-reader, zoom/reflow, high-contrast and reduced-motion judgments. Agent and synthetic checks certify only their recorded scopes. No pilot results or outreach are claimed.

## Delivery and retained gates

Source, course-package transport, application package, installed and human states remain distinct on every entry. Mapping does not build/install/commit anything. The historical October 4 aggregate remains partial: 219/221 executed Python scripts, 224 discovered with three build-dependent scripts deferred, JS passed, two advertised-LAN scripts and dirty-tree failed. On October 7 the unchanged exact fixed-response precheck succeeded in all three loopback and all three advertised-route modes. The two genuine scripts, `tests/daemon_roundtrip.py` and `tests/model_phase_roundtrip.py`, then passed against that route on the pinned pre-P13 source. Their original failures are retained. This is a resolved route boundary at that snapshot, not a new combined aggregate or clean release.

Q1-Q4 exact choices, submission provenance, workspace/root policy and human/learning acceptance retain their existing explicit decisions. Independent ready work may continue without choosing them. Full aggregate, CI-only, clean candidate and final package/installed gates must be run only when their actual source/delivery conditions justify them. Git, installation, release, external messaging, live paid providers and private learner-file changes have no new authorization.

## Per-entry accountability

The exact heading link names the preserved original; the interpretation below is visibly derived. Existing dispositions are reported, not newly assigned. No real learner content, key or score is accepted by this audit. Package/installed/human fields mean current-source acceptance unless a row explicitly names a dated narrower receipt.

### V68: stacked arguments and equivalent depth across subjects

Original and applicable interpretation: [2026-09-15: stacked arguments and equivalent depth across subjects](../../USER-VISION.md#2026-09-15-stacked-arguments-and-equivalent-depth-across-subjects). Raw lines 2623-2668; quote-line count 2 (parser count, subject to caveats above).

**Derived interpretation and relationships:** accountability as of: 2026-10-07; current meaning status and relationships: Original complete dated meanings/statuses/relationships above remain preserved. Current recognizer requires explicit quoted argument and therefore/thus/hence conclusion; decimal/ambiguous-abbreviation cases fall back. Historical September15 installed repair and September18 source reconciliation exist.; applicable meanings: update: Interpretation recorded 2026-09-15; meaning: a learner should not have to unpack a logical, symbolic or procedural structure from one dense paragraph when its parts are already identifiable. Present premises, conclusions, derivation steps, code lines, events, speaker turns, procedure steps, comparison dimensions and language layers as separately scannable units. Use bullets, labeled rows, mathematical display, code blocks, timelines, tables or paired fields according to the content's actual structure.  The shared rule is semantic, not subject decoration. Preserve the author's or source's exact words and order. Enhance explicit structure and deliberate line breaks. Do not make a renderer guess roles from ordinary prose. Every rich form keeps a coherent plain, narrow-screen, keyboard and screen-reader path.; applicable relationships: extends the September 12 code-box and thematic-environment entries from one programming example to a shared rule for arguments and other structured content. It also applies the accepted Phase 16 capability contract to quiz stimuli without creating a new scorer, parser or subject-specific assessment authority.

### V72: progressive question emphasis and hint augmentation

Original and applicable interpretation: [2026-09-16: progressive question emphasis and hint augmentation](../../USER-VISION.md#2026-09-16-progressive-question-emphasis-and-hint-augmentation). Raw lines 2772-2850; quote-line count 1 (parser count, subject to caveats above).

**Derived interpretation and relationships:** accountability as of: 2026-10-07; current meaning status and relationships: Original complete dated meanings/statuses/relationships above remain preserved. Both dated interpretations preserved: exploratory roles then implemented stable-prompt disclosed-cue layer. Current layer renders teaching.shown only; locked tiers remain separate.; applicable meanings: update: Interpretation recorded 2026-09-16; meaning: certain questions should present their meaningful structure more clearly through semantic text roles, restrained color, spacing, labels, and fitting content layouts. When the runtime grants a hint, the same question may gain an additional teaching layer in place, such as an inline definition, a marked condition, a grouped argument, a diagram annotation, or a cue attached to the relevant clause. The original wording stays available and does not silently change beneath the learner.  The useful unit is not arbitrary rich text. Authors identify semantic roles such as task, given information, constraint, negation, comparison dimension, speaker, code, or conclusion. Presentation maps those roles to accessible styles. Color may reinforce a role but cannot carry it alone. Hint-triggered augmentation renders only content the runtime has disclosed and remains distinguishable from the authored question.; update: Prototype result recorded 2026-09-16; meaning: disclosed runtime cues now accumulate inside an open Assisted question layer directly below the unchanged prompt. The learner can collapse that layer to return to the original-only view. The next locked cue remains in Help, visibly separate from what has already been disclosed. Tier names and text provide the meaning. Color, border weight, and pattern only reinforce those labels.; applicable relationships: extends the September 12 thematic quiz and code-box entries, the September 15 semantic-structure entry, and the September 16 symbol-help and content-specific formatting entries. It makes the earlier bright-highlighting direction concrete inside questions while preserving the fixed hint order and the rule that authors select semantic roles rather than raw colors.; implements the first reversible slice of this entry through IL-20260916-01. It preserves the prior semantic-structure, symbol-help, fixed-tier, and runtime-disclosure decisions.

### V73: beneficial depth levels and code-question audit

Original and applicable interpretation: [2026-09-16: beneficial depth levels and code-question audit](../../USER-VISION.md#2026-09-16-beneficial-depth-levels-and-code-question-audit). Raw lines 2851-2896; quote-line count 1 (parser count, subject to caveats above).

**Derived interpretation and relationships:** accountability as of: 2026-10-07; current meaning status and relationships: Original complete dated meanings/statuses/relationships above remain preserved. D1/D2 disposable comparison, separate JS/Python Dojo and one native coding unit exist. Higher depth is not automatically better, difficulty/mastery/hint tier or editor parity.; applicable meanings: update: Interpretation recorded 2026-09-16; meaning: content-specific question support may progress through levels when the target task warrants it. For code, the useful sequence runs from faithful formatted source, through orientation and state tracing, to small manipulation and finally construction or transfer. Higher is not automatically better. The optimal treatment is the least costly level that lets the learner perform the intended thinking and diagnose a meaningful error.  The levels describe presentation and learner action, not item difficulty, mastery, correctness, or hint entitlement. A concept-choice item may stop at a readable code block. An output-prediction item may add a learner-controlled trace. A debugging or construction task may need an editor and deterministic checks. The original source and a useful static representation remain available at every level.; applicable relationships: extends the September 12 code-box and CS Dojo directions, the September 15 cross-subject structure audit, and the September 16 progressive question-assistance entry. It narrows "more" into a testable optimality rule and prevents richer code tooling from becoming the default for every computer-science question.
